Verstappen claimed Red Bull had also dropped behind [Ferrari](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Scuderia_Ferrari) and [Mercedes](25098794.md), as Lawson was replaced by [Yuki Tsunoda](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Yuki_Tsunoda).[^cite_note-295][^cite_note-296] He received widespread acclaim for his surprise pole position at the [Japanese Grand Prix](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2025_Japanese_Grand_Prix),[^cite_note-JPN2025-297] before holding off both McLaren drivers to claim his first victory of the year;[^cite_note-298] Andrew Benson of *[BBC Sport](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/BBC_Sport)* described it as "a weekend of the sort of rare perfection that comes only from drivers of the very highest quality".[^cite_note-299] He finished sixth in [Bahrain](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2025_Bahrain_Grand_Prix) amidst further performance and [pit stop](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pit_stop) struggles, prompting Red Bull to hold crisis talks over his future.[^cite_note-300] He returned to pole in [Saudi Arabia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2025_Saudi_Arabian_Grand_Prix), where he finished second after receiving a penalty for a first-corner incident with Piastri.[^cite_note-301] Verstappen finished fourth in the [Miami](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2025_Miami_Grand_Prix) sprint, demoted to seventeenth following a penalty for an unsafe pit release;[^cite_note-302] he took pole for the main race, ultimately finishing fourth.[^cite_note-303]

Verstappen driving the Red Bull RB21 at the 2025 Emilia Romagna Grand Prix

Verstappen won the [Emilia Romagna Grand Prix](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2025_Emilia_Romagna_Grand_Prix) after a lauded first-lap overtake on championship-leader [Oscar Piastri](59449712.md).

Verstappen won the [Emilia Romagna Grand Prix](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2025_Emilia_Romagna_Grand_Prix) after a first-lap overtake on Piastri into *[Variante Tamburello](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Imola_Circuit#Tamburello)*, which was widely hailed as one of his greatest.[^cite_note-EMI2025-304] His fourth-place in [Monaco](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2025_Monaco_Grand_Prix) was followed by tenth in [Spain](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2025_Spanish_Grand_Prix), where he was penalised for an intentional collision with [George Russell](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/George_Russell_\(racing_driver\)) as he fell out of contention for the victory;[^cite_note-305][^cite_note-306] the pair [finished 1–2](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Glossary_of_motorsport_terms#0%E2%80%939) in [Canada](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2025_Canadian_Grand_Prix), with Verstappen second.[^cite_note-307] After qualifying seventh for the [Austrian Grand Prix](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2025_Austrian_Grand_Prix), Verstappen retired after being hit by [Kimi Antonelli](70530918.md) on the first lap;[^cite_note-308] 61 points behind championship leader Piastri, Verstappen admitted his fifth title challenge was "case closed".[^cite_note-309] He took another pole at the [British Grand Prix](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2025_British_Grand_Prix) on a low-[downforce](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Downforce) setup, before ceding the lead to Piastri and spinning during a [safety car](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Safety_car) restart in wet-weather conditions; he recovered to fifth and criticised his lack of [grip](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Glossary_of_motorsport_terms#G).[^cite_note-310] He won the [Belgium](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2025_Belgian_Grand_Prix) sprint after a first-lap overtake on Piastri and claimed fourth in the Grand Prix.[^cite_note-311] He dropped to ninth in [Hungary](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2025_Hungarian_Grand_Prix) before finishing second to Piastri at the [Dutch Grand Prix](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2025_Dutch_Grand_Prix), dropping him 104 points behind the Australian McLaren driver who led the points standings after that race and 70 points behind Norris who retired from the race with power issues.[^cite_note-312][^cite_note-313]

Verstappen's pole lap in [Italy](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2025_Italian_Grand_Prix) was the [fastest in Formula One history](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_Formula_One_race_records),[^cite_note-314] where he overcame early opposition from Norris to win the Grand Prix by over 19 seconds in record speed.[^cite_note-315] He claimed a *[grand chelem](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Glossary_of_motorsport_terms#G)* with his 14-second victory at the [Azerbaijan Grand Prix](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2025_Azerbaijan_Grand_Prix), before finishing second to Russell in [Singapore](77743466.md). His back-to-back victories in the [United States](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2025_United_States_Grand_Prix) sprint and Grand Prix reduced his deficit to 40 points, and he recovered from fifth to third in [Mexico City](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2025_Mexico_City_Grand_Prix) with a [one-stop strategy](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Glossary_of_motorsport_terms#0%E2%80%939). Finishing fourth from sixth at the [São Paulo](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2025_S%C3%A3o_Paulo_Grand_Prix) sprint, Verstappen qualified sixteenth for the Grand Prix, his lowest qualifying position – excluding any penalties – since the [2021 Russian Grand Prix](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2021_Russian_Grand_Prix), which he chose not to run any qualifying laps for due to impending engine and grid penalties. Red Bull started him from the pit lane after changing his engine in *[parc fermé](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Parc_ferm%C3%A9)*; surviving an early puncture, he climbed up to third in the race to draw ever closer to the championship lead. Verstappen won at [Las Vegas](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2025_Las_Vegas_Grand_Prix) with both McLarens being disqualified post-race, moving him level on points with Piastri and 24 behind championship leader Norris. Verstappen finished fourth in the [Qatar](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2025_Qatar_Grand_Prix) sprint before improving to collect a win in the Grand Prix, bringing him twelve points behind Norris and overtaking Piastri for second in the championship. Converting pole position in [Abu Dhabi](77743503.md) to a win, he achieved the most Grand Prix wins throughout the season with eight overall, with both McLaren drivers winning seven apiece. He ultimately placed merely two points behind Norris, who secured the Drivers' Championship.

#### 2026: New regulations



Verstappen qualifying for the [2026 Chinese Grand Prix](80284337.md)

Verstappen was partnered with [Isack Hadjar](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Isack_Hadjar) for the [2026 season](71681684.md). Ahead of the season, Verstappen announced he would run the number 3 on his car as the FIA granted drivers permission to change their number ahead of 2026, instead of reverting to his old number 33.[^cite_note-316]

Verstappen started the season with a crash in qualifying at the [Australian Grand Prix](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2026_Australian_Grand_Prix),[^cite_note-317] where he recovered to sixth after battling defending champion [Lando Norris](48978813.md).[^cite_note-318] At the [Chinese Grand Prix](80284337.md), Verstappen qualified in eighth for both the sprint[^cite_note-319] and Grand Prix, and recorded no points in both races, suffering an engine-related retirement during the Grand Prix.[^cite_note-320][^cite_note-321] Ahead of the [Japanese Grand Prix](80284355.md), Verstappen refused to answer media questions until *[The Guardian](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Guardian)* journalist Giles Richards was removed, following Richards's pointed questioning [in Abu Dhabi](77743503.md) regarding the [2025 Spanish Grand Prix](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2025_Spanish_Grand_Prix).[^cite_note-322] Following an eighth-place finish in Japan, Verstappen told [BBC Sport](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/BBC_Sport) that he was considering retirement following the 2026 season.[^cite_note-323] At the [Miami Grand Prix](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2026_Miami_Grand_Prix), Verstappen finished fifth in both the sprint and the main race.[^cite_note-324] He placed seventh in the [Canadian Grand Prix](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2026_Canadian_Grand_Prix) sprint, before qualifying sixth and finishing third in the main race.[^cite_note-325] Qualifying on the front row in [Monaco](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2026_Monaco_Grand_Prix), he retired on the opening lap following power unit struggles that dropped him to last off the line.[^cite_note-326] Verstappen finished fourth at [Barcelona-Catalunya](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2026_Barcelona-Catalunya_Grand_Prix),[^cite_note-327] before returning to the podium at the [Austrian Grand Prix](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2026_Austrian_Grand_Prix).[^cite_note-328] At [Silverstone](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2026_British_Grand_Prix), he placed sixth in the sprint race and qualified seventh for the main race.[^cite_note-329][^cite_note-330] He crashed on the forty-eighth lap of the main race, leading to a safety car finish for the race.[^cite_note-331] Verstappen returned to the podium again at the [Belgian Grand Prix](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2026_Belgian_Grand_Prix), where he qualified second and finished third.[^cite_note-332][^cite_note-333]

In August 2026, Verstappen signed a contract extension with Red Bull Racing until the 2030 season.[^cite_note-334] At the [Dutch Grand Prix](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2026_Dutch_Grand_Prix), he finished sixth in the sprint race,[^cite_note-335] but did not finish the main race after crashing on the opening lap.[^cite_note-336]

At the [Azerbaijan Grand Prix](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2026_Azerbaijan_Grand_Prix), Verstappen qualified eighth but quickly charged through the field, finishing second by just 0.196 seconds to [George Russell](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/George_Russell_\(racing_driver\)), leading the Red Bull team's first double podium since the [2024 Chinese Grand Prix](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2024_Chinese_Grand_Prix) alongside teammate [Isack Hadjar](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Isack_Hadjar).[^cite_note-337]

## Driver profile

### Qualifying pace and consistency



Verstappen on a qualifying lap at the [2018 Chinese Grand Prix](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2018_Chinese_Grand_Prix)

Following Verstappen's maiden pole position at the 2019 Hungarian Grand Prix, former Formula One World Champion Nico Rosberg described him as the fastest driver in Formula One. Rosberg elucidated that Verstappen did not have the fastest car during the qualifying session in Hungary, emphasising that it was Verstappen's raw speed that allowed him to secure pole position: "Mercedes still had the fastest car, and it's just Verstappen with his driving that put it up there in pole."[^cite_note-338]

In 2019, former Formula One World Champion [Jenson Button](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jenson_Button) stated that he believes Verstappen is the fastest Formula One driver ever: "I think he is the fastest driver that has ever driven an F1 car. I really do, I think he is unbelievably fast."[^cite_note-339] Journalist Scott Mitchell from *The Race* commented: "When everything comes together, Verstappen is the quickest driver in F1 over one lap, but the odd mistake and scruffy session must be eliminated."[^cite_note-340] Helmut Marko, the advisor to the [Red Bull GmbH](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Red_Bull_GmbH) Formula One teams, stated that Verstappen is the fastest driver Red Bull have ever had.[^cite_note-341][^cite_note-342]

In 2021, three-time Formula One World Champion [Jackie Stewart](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jackie_Stewart) claimed that Verstappen is the fastest driver in Formula One, but he also added that Verstappen is still in the "puppy stage" of his career.[^cite_note-343] [Peter Windsor](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Peter_Windsor), an experienced Formula One journalist, expressed Verstappen's ability to change direction "quicker than anybody else" through the high-speed section Maggots and Becketts: Silverstone's fastest sequence of corners. Windsor stressed that Verstappen's lightning-quick change of direction is a result of the innate feel he has for creating the "perfect platform" to achieve what he wants with the car during a lap. He is intrinsically able to create, for "a trillionth of a second", a minuscule "flat area" between the left and the right of a change of direction ahead of him, in order to attain a stable balance with the car before applying the next steering and throttle input.[^cite_note-344] [Scuderia AlphaTauri](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Scuderia_AlphaTauri)'s team principal, [Franz Tost](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Franz_Tost), declared Verstappen to be the fastest driver in Formula One; following the Dutchman's "really deserved" maiden title triumph in 2021.[^cite_note-Woodhouse2021-345] After winning the [2024](72504121.md) World Drivers' Championship in the third-placed [RB20](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Red_Bull_RB20), James Elson of *[Motor Sport](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Motor_Sport_\(magazine\))* stated "if there was any doubt beforehand, 2024 proved that Max Verstappen is F1's best driver", adding that "not only the fastest, capable of creating one-lap magic, he knows how to produce all it takes to win in the race too".[^cite_note-346] Verstappen received widespread acclaim for his surprise pole lap at the [2025 Japanese Grand Prix](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2025_Japanese_Grand_Prix),[^cite_note-JPN2025-297] with Andrew Benson of *[BBC Sport](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/BBC_Sport)* commenting that "many F1 observers regarded \[Verstappen's qualifying lap] as one of the greatest of all time".[^cite_note-347]

### Dominance over teammates



Verstappen *(right)* was teammates with [Daniel Ricciardo](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Daniel_Ricciardo) *(left)* from [2016](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2016_Formula_One_World_Championship) to [2018](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2018_Formula_One_World_Championship), pictured at the [2016 Malaysian Grand Prix](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2016_Malaysian_Grand_Prix).

&#x20;Former Formula One driver [Eddie Irvine](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Eddie_Irvine) praised Verstappen, stating that he is "by far the most dominant team leader on the grid." Irvine highlighted that Verstappen "has had many different second drivers in the team and none of them have got close to him."[^cite_note-Sharan2021Irvine-348] During the 2020 season, Jenson Button stated that he does not "think there's been a driver that's annihilated their teammates like he \[Verstappen] has in a very long time."[^cite_note-349] Motorsport columnist and former Formula One driver [Jolyon Palmer](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jolyon_Palmer), commented that Verstappen's maiden World Championship triumph could usher in a "new era of dominance" in the sport. Palmer wrote:

> \[Verstappen's] qualifying pace is frightening and has been the basis for this title-charging campaign. He's had the most pole positions of anyone with 10 – almost half of the races, and it would have been half had he completed his mesmeric Jeddah lap, which ended up being possibly Max's biggest error in a near-perfect season. For a driver who has demolished three successive team mates in three years and made only a handful of small mistakes in that time, you question what scope their \[*[sic](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sic)*] even is for improvement – but if he can take his game onto another level from here, we could be about to see the start of a new era of dominance.[^cite_note-350]

Journalist Scott Mitchell expounded the reason why the Dutchman's teammates have fallen short "even with the data". Mitchell wrote: "Verstappen drives mainly on intuition, and that comes from years of training and preparation. He has a database in his head which he can use immediately. Even with data, Gasly and Albon could see where he was faster, but they could never grasp how he was faster there. That's because what makes Verstappen so good is mainly unconscious." According to Mitchell, those years of training and driving on his intuition has made it impossible for other drivers to measure up to Verstappen.[^cite_note-351]



Verstappen *(right)* was teammates with [Sergio Pérez](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sergio_P%C3%A9rez) *(left)* from 2021 to 2024, pictured at the [2024 Chinese Grand Prix](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2024_Chinese_Grand_Prix).

Helmut Marko asserted that Verstappen's progress has moved him clear of his former Formula 1 team-mate Daniel Ricciardo, in both qualifying and race trim. "There is specific data," Marko told *Autosport*, "In qualifying the gap to Ricciardo is greater. The strange thing is Max sometimes slides more, but still manages to keep the tyres alive." Verstappen previously mentioned that he was three or four-tenths faster on average than "very fast qualifier" Daniel Ricciardo.[^cite_note-352] Marko concluded that then 20-year-old Verstappen "clearly moved away" from Ricciardo since the "second half of the 2018 season."[^cite_note-353]

### Natural talent

Max Verstappen driving the Red Bull RB16B (2021)

Max Verstappen driving the Red Bull RB18 (2022)

Max Verstappen driving the Red Bull RB19 (2023)

Max Verstappen driving the Red Bull RB20 (2024)

Verstappen's four championship-winning cars from top-to-bottom: [RB16B](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Red_Bull_RB16B) ([2021](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2021_Formula_One_World_Championship)), [RB18](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Red_Bull_RB18) ([2022](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2022_Formula_One_World_Championship)), [RB19](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Red_Bull_RB19) ([2023](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2023_Formula_One_World_Championship)) and [RB20](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Red_Bull_RB20) ([2024](72504121.md))

Following his maiden Formula One win, at the [2016 Spanish Grand Prix](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2016_Spanish_Grand_Prix), three-time World Drivers' Champion [Niki Lauda](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Niki_Lauda) described Verstappen as the "talent of the century".[^cite_note-354] Red Bull team principal Christian Horner stated that Verstappen is the best driver Red Bull have ever seen: "I have no doubt \[Verstappen is] the best we have seen on one of our cars, in terms of outright raw ability and commitment. He's the best driver we've seen."[^cite_note-355]

[Gerhard Berger](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gerhard_Berger) echoed [2009](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2009_Formula_One_World_Championship) World Drivers' Champion [Jenson Button](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jenson_Button)'s comments, anointing Max Verstappen as the Formula One driver of this era with the most raw talent. Speaking to *Sport1*, Berger ventured: "When it comes to raw talent, Max Verstappen is the most talented of all. But it's not just about raw talent, it's about the whole package. That means it's not just about setting the fastest laps but also to win a race. And not just \[about] one race but many and eventually a championship, and championships."[^cite_note-356]

Two-time World Drivers' Champion [Fernando Alonso](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fernando_Alonso) commented that Verstappen is the standout driver in Formula One, declaring him to be "one step ahead" of everyone. Alonso noted that Verstappen deserved to win the [2021 Formula One World Championship](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2021_Formula_One_World_Championship), ahead of the title decider that saw Verstappen and [Lewis Hamilton](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lewis_Hamilton) enter level on points: "Mercedes lately have been more performing and they've won a couple of races now, but Max is driving—in my opinion—one step ahead of all of us. We saw the \[qualifying] lap in Jeddah, until he touched the wall at the last corner, that lap was coming from Max, not the Red Bull."[^cite_note-357] [Karun Chandhok](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Karun_Chandhok) likened Verstappen's ruthless and uncompromising attitude in race combat to seven-time World Drivers' Champion [Michael Schumacher](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Michael_Schumacher).[^cite_note-358] By [2025](72768531.md), Andrew Benson of *[BBC Sport](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/BBC_Sport)* opined that Verstappen was "regarded by almost everyone in \[Formula One] as the best driver in the world".[^cite_note-359]

### Rivalries

#### Lewis Hamilton



Verstappen (left) and Hamilton (right) at the [2022 French Grand Prix](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2022_French_Grand_Prix)

Verstappen's most well-known rival is his [2021](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2021_Formula_One_World_Championship) title rival, [Lewis Hamilton](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lewis_Hamilton).[^cite_note-360][^cite_note-361] The title battle between Verstappen and Hamilton in 2021 has been compared to other legendary battles like the [Prost–Senna rivalry](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Prost%E2%80%93Senna_rivalry).[^cite_note-362] The two swapped leads five times, finished 1–2 in fourteen out of 22 races, and numerous incidents on-track involving the two[^cite_note-366] culminated in a title decider at the season finale, the [Abu Dhabi Grand Prix](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2021_Abu_Dhabi_Grand_Prix), where Verstappen, benefitting from newer tyres that were fitted during a safety car period, passed Hamilton on the final lap for the lead, and ultimately the championship.

#### Charles Leclerc



Verstappen (left), Leclerc (middle) and Hamilton (right) on the podium at the [2022 Austrian Grand Prix](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2022_Austrian_Grand_Prix)

[Charles Leclerc](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Charles_Leclerc) has also been cited as one of Verstappen's rivals. Following a [junior karting](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kart_racing) incident in 2012 where Leclerc brushed off a collision with Verstappen as "just an incident", a rivalry began between the two which has since been matured into mutual respect. Verstappen's battles with Leclerc are characterised as cleaner and more respectful, focusing on "pure racing".[^cite_note-367] Verstappen has noted he treats Leclerc differently than other rivals, simply because he has known him and trusted his racing ability.[^cite_note-368][^cite_note-369] Verstappen and Leclerc battled for the championship at the beginning of [2022](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2022_Formula_One_World_Championship) before Ferrari's performance declined, allowing Red Bull and Verstappen to eventually secure both championships.

### Racecraft



Verstappen *(middle)* on the podium with [Lewis Hamilton](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lewis_Hamilton) *(left)* and [Daniel Ricciardo](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Daniel_Ricciardo) *(right)* after winning the [2017 Malaysian Grand Prix](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2017_Malaysian_Grand_Prix)

Following Verstappen being given a warning for his driving conduct at the 2016 Belgian Grand Prix, the 18-year-old received the backing of then three-time champion Lewis Hamilton, who commented: "firstly, give the guy a break, he is 18 years old. What the frickin' heck were any of us doing at 18?"[^cite_note-370]

Despite earning high praise and acclaim from Formula One drivers and experts in and out of the sport, Verstappen faced criticism in the wake of the penalties he incurred during the [2021 Saudi Arabian Grand Prix](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2021_Saudi_Arabian_Grand_Prix). Following this race, former Formula One driver [Martin Brundle](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Martin_Brundle) wrote: "Such is \[his] car control and cunning, he's sometimes able to pull off the audacious moves and leave a margin of doubt as to whether it's hard racing or simply a professional foul outside of the regulations and it's those moments which are generating the confusion, controversies and inconsistencies. [Ayrton Senna](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ayrton_Senna) and Michael Schumacher had their faults too, and I was on the receiving end from both of them on occasions, but it's a sizeable dent on their immense reputations, not a positive."[^cite_note-371] Following an impressive first half of the 2022 season, journalist [David Tremayne](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/David_Tremayne) claimed Verstappen had elevated himself to a new higher level compared to his 2021 title winning season, suggesting Verstappen's first title had made him more relaxed and "chirpy".[^cite_note-372]

Following the [2024 Mexico City Grand Prix](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2024_Mexico_City_Grand_Prix), Verstappen received widespread condemnation from fans and media for incidents involving title rival [Lando Norris](48978813.md), for which he received 20 seconds worth of time penalties.[^cite_note-373] Many commentators and former drivers likened his tactics and 'win-at-all-costs' attitude to those of Senna and Schumacher,[^cite_note-374][^cite_note-375] whilst [1996](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/1996_Formula_One_World_Championship) World Drivers' Champion [Damon Hill](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Damon_Hill) characterised them as reminiscent of *[Wacky Races](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wacky_Races)* antagonist [Dick Dastardly](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dick_Dastardly).[^cite_note-376] Before the following race in [São Paulo](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2024_S%C3%A3o_Paulo_Grand_Prix), Verstappen criticised the alleged British bias in the media, stating "I have the wrong passport".[^cite_note-377] Upon his victory from 17th on the grid in the rain-affected race, he stated in the post-race press conference "I appreciate all of you being here, but I don't see any British press. Do they have to run to the airport, or \[do they not] know where the press conference is?"[^cite_note-378]

### Fan support



The Verstappen grandstand at the [2021 Austrian Grand Prix](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2021_Austrian_Grand_Prix)

Verstappen has accumulated a significant fanbase from around the world, particularly from his home country of the Netherlands. The frequent association of Verstappen with the colour [orange](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Orange_\(colour\))—the [national colour](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/National_colour) of the Netherlands—has led to his fanbase being referred to as the *Orange Army*.[^cite_note-379][^cite_note-380] Several races across Europe have dedicated Verstappen [grandstands](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Grandstand) to accommodate thousands of travelling Dutch fans, boosting ticket sales at Grands Prix such as in [Belgium](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Belgian_Grand_Prix), [Austria](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Austrian_Grand_Prix) and [Hungary](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hungarian_Grand_Prix).[^cite_note-Smith_2018-381] A Verstappen grandstand featured at the inaugural [Las Vegas Grand Prix](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Las_Vegas_Grand_Prix) in [2023](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2023_Las_Vegas_Grand_Prix).[^cite_note-382]

During the [2021 Dutch Grand Prix](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2021_Dutch_Grand_Prix), the managing director of [Honda in Formula One](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Honda_in_Formula_One), [Masashi Yamamoto](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Masashi_Yamamoto_\(motorsport\)?action=edit\&redlink=1), praised Verstappen's support, stating that "in the six years I've been involved with Honda F1, I've never seen such a great turnout as at the Dutch Grand Prix. It was like being in a football stadium."[^cite_note-gpblog.com_2022-383] In October 2021, Verstappen was voted the most popular Formula One driver in an official worldwide survey conducted by [Nielsen Media Research](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nielsen_Media_Research) and [Motorsport Network](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Motorsport_Network), earning 14.4% of the vote with particular success in the [United States](3434750.md) and [Japan](15573.md).[^cite_note-Galloway_2021-384][^cite_note-385]

The fan-made song "[Super Max!](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Super_Max!)", performed by the [Pitstop Boys](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pitstop_Boys?action=edit\&redlink=1) in 2016, went viral after his victory at the [2021 Abu Dhabi Grand Prix](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2021_Abu_Dhabi_Grand_Prix), topping the [Spotify](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Spotify) *Viral 50* chart in the Netherlands and reaching number two in the United Kingdom.[^cite_note-Gamble_2021-386] Another fan-made [EDM](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Electronic_dance_music) track "33 Max Verstappen", was released in 2023, eventually going viral on social media due to [internet memes](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Internet_meme) referencing the hook: "tu tu tu du, Max Verstappen" (sometimes also written as "du du du du, Max Verstappen").[^cite_note-387][^cite_note-388]

## Other racing

### Sportscar racing



The [Acura ARX-06](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Acura_ARX-06) (pictured in 2023) tested by Verstappen with [Honda Racing Corporation](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Honda_Racing_Corporation) in November 2024.

Verstappen tested the [Honda NSX-GT](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Honda_NSX_\(second_generation\)#Super_GT)—a [GT500](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Super_GT#GT500) class [Super GT](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Super_GT) car—during the 2022 [Honda Racing](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Honda_Racing_Corporation) *Thanks Day* event at [Motegi](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mobility_Resort_Motegi).[^cite_note-389] After winning the event's [kart race](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kart_racing) alongside [Marc Márquez](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Marc_M%C3%A1rquez), Honda awarded Verstappen—the first [Honda-powered](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Honda_in_Formula_One) World Drivers' Champion since [1991](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/1991_Formula_One_World_Championship)—with an [NSX Type S](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Honda_NSX_\(second_generation\)#NSX_Type_S_\(2022\)).[^cite_note-390] He later tested a Red Bull–liveried [Nissan Z NISMO GT500](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nissan_Z_\(RZ34\)#Super_GT) at [Fuji](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fuji_Speedway) prior to the [2026 Japanese Grand Prix](80284355.md).[^cite_note-391]

In January 2024, Verstappen drove the [Ferrari 296 GT3](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ferrari_296_GT3) at the [Portimão](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Algarve_International_Circuit), as part of a two-day test with [Deutsche Tourenwagen Masters](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Deutsche_Tourenwagen_Masters) team [Emil Frey Racing](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Emil_Frey_Racing) alongside [Thierry Vermeulen](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Thierry_Vermeulen),[^cite_note-392] who competed under the *Verstappen.com Racing* banner in [2023](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2023_Deutsche_Tourenwagen_Masters).[^cite_note-393] Team principal Lorenz Frey-Hilti stated "there were also other [GT3](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Group_GT3) cars that day, but I can only say that Verstappen was driving on a different level than the rest. There are good drivers who cannot shine in a GT3 car due to a lack of experience, but Max is different. If something has four wheels, then \[he's fast in it]", adding that he was "world-class".[^cite_note-394]

On 19 November 2024, Verstappen drove a [prototype](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sports_prototype) for the first time, testing the [Acura ARX-06](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Acura_ARX-06) with [Honda Racing Corporation](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Honda_Racing_Corporation) at [Las Vegas Motor Speedway](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Las_Vegas_Motor_Speedway), ahead of the [Las Vegas Grand Prix](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2024_Las_Vegas_Grand_Prix).[^cite_note-395] Verstappen expressed his desire to compete in the [24 Hours of Daytona](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/24_Hours_of_Daytona) and [Le Mans](1401596.md) after the test, stating that "it's just about finding the time".[^cite_note-396]

In May 2025, he entered testing for the third round of the [2025 Nürburgring Langstrecken-Serie](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2025_N%C3%BCrburgring_Langstrecken-Serie) in the 296 under the [pseudonym](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pseudonym) "Franz Hermann", where he claimed to set an unofficial [GT3](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Group_GT3) lap record, reportedly in an attempt to obtain a licence to compete on the *[Nordschleife](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nordschleife)*.[^cite_note-397][^cite_note-398] He received an [FIA Platinum](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/FIA_Platinum) licence later that month, allowing him to compete in top-level [endurance racing](699018.md).[^cite_note-399]

In September 2025, he made his competitive debut on the *Nordschleife* in the seventh round for [Lionspeed GP](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lionspeed_GP), completing the laps required for a GT3 permit in a [horsepower](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Engine_power)-restricted [Porsche 718 Cayman GT4 CS](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_718_Boxster_and_Cayman_\(982\)#718_Cayman_GT4_and_718_Spyder) as he finished seventh in-class.[^cite_note-400] Later the same month, Verstappen made his GT3 race debut and won. He drove for [Emil Frey Racing](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Emil_Frey_Racing), racing a [Ferrari 296 GT3](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ferrari_296#Ferrari_296_GT3) with [Chris Lulham](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Chris_Lulham) in the [Nürburgring Endurance Series](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/N%C3%BCrburgring_Langstrecken-Serie).[^cite_note-401] This start also made Max Verstappen the first defending Formula One World Champion to simultaneously compete in Formula One and another racing series since [Keke Rosberg](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Keke_Rosberg), who made a one-off appearance in the [1983 World Sportscar Championship](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/1983_World_Sportscar_Championship), also at the Nürburgring Nordschleife. After qualifying third, Verstappen, who drove the first stint, passed two cars and began to create a gap of over a minute. Verstappen handed the car to teammate Lulham for the second stint; Lulham crossed the line to win the race for the team, 24 seconds ahead of the car in second place.[^cite_note-402]

In preparation for his participation at the [2026 24 Hours of Nürburgring](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2026_24_Hours_of_N%C3%BCrburgring), Verstappen took part in the second round of the [2026 Nürburgring Langstrecken-Serie](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2026_N%C3%BCrburgring_Langstrecken-Serie), driving a [Mercedes-AMG GT3 Evo](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mercedes-AMG_GT#AMG_GT3_Evo_\(2020%E2%80%93present\)) run by [Winward Racing](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Winward_Racing).[^cite_note-AMG2026-403] In a qualifying session affected by multiple [code 60 sections](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Code_60_flag), Verstappen put together a clean lap and achieved his maiden NLS pole position.[^cite_note-404] After fighting for the lead with [Christopher Haase](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Christopher_Haase) throughout the opening stint, the No. 3 Verstappen Mercedes-AMG broke away from the field, allowing Verstappen, [Daniel Juncadella](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Daniel_Juncadella), and [Jules Gounon](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jules_Gounon) to win by almost a minute.[^cite_note-405][^cite_note-406] Following the race, the team was disqualified for using seven sets of tyres, which exceeded the limit of six allowed for race day.[^cite_note-407][^cite_note-408]

### Sim racing

In his free time, Verstappen competes in [sim racing](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sim_racing). Verstappen stated that it helps him with his real-life racing, keeping him "ready to go".[^cite_note-RacingNews365-409] In 2015, he became a member of [Team Redline](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Team_Redline), a European professional sim racing team.[^cite_note-410][^cite_note-411] On 23 March 2026, he absorbed the team into his wider Verstappen Racing motorsport project, rebranding the esports organization to [Verstappen Sim Racing](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Verstappen_Sim_Racing). As part of the rebrand, the team now also serves as a driver development pathway from virtual racing into real-world competition.[^cite_note-412]

He has also gained notoriety from critics for his sim racing activities clashing with Grand Prix weekends in some cases, most notably in the [2024 Hungarian Grand Prix](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2024_Hungarian_Grand_Prix), where he criticised [Red Bull Racing](1173193.md)'s strategy calls and the [RB20](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Red_Bull_Racing_RB20)'s lack of performance over team radio. Verstappen had taken part in an *iRacing* sim racing event prior to the Grand Prix, racing until 3\:00 a.m.[^cite_note-413]

#### iRacing special events (2020–present)

See also: [iRacing](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/IRacing)

Verstappen has competed in several marquee events on *[iRacing](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/IRacing)* since February 2019, when he entered the *[Bathurst](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mount_Panorama) 12 Special Event*—a 12-hour virtual team [endurance race](699018.md)—alongside [Lando Norris](48978813.md).[^cite_note-414] The pair won the *[Spa](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Spa-Francorchamps) 24 Special Event* in July.[^cite_note-415] Verstappen won the *[Petit Le Mans](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Petit_Le_Mans) Special Event* in October 2020.[^cite_note-416] Later that year, [Haas](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Haas_F1_Team) driver and *R8G eSports* founder [Romain Grosjean](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Romain_Grosjean) deemed Verstappen to be the "fastest" driver in sim racing.[^cite_note-417] He entered the eighth round of the 2023 *AMX Global League* at [Imola](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Imola_Circuit), winning the first heat.[^cite_note-ATK-418]

Verstappen entered the *[Daytona](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Daytona_International_Speedway) 24 Special Event* in January 2024, winning the [GTD](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/IMSA_SportsCar_Championship#GT_Daytona_\(GTD\)) class with Redline.[^cite_note-419] In May, Verstappen received media attention for entering the *[Nürburgring](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/N%C3%BCrburgring) 24 Special Event* on the same weekend as the [Emilia Romagna Grand Prix](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2024_Emilia_Romagna_Grand_Prix);[^cite_note-420] he won both events, achieving a [record-equalling](13599687.md#Most_consecutive_pole_positions) eighth consecutive pole position at the latter. Following his victory at the former, Ed Hardy of *[Autosport](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Autosport)* opined that Verstappen had "firmly established himself as one of the best sim racers in the world".[^cite_note-ATK-418] Verstappen won his second *Spa 24 Special Event* in July that year, completing a perfect season of 24-hour special events.[^cite_note-421]

#### Le Mans Virtual (2020–2023)

See also: [24 Hours of Le Mans Virtual](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/24_Hours_of_Le_Mans_Virtual), [Le Mans Virtual Series](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Le_Mans_Virtual_Series), and [rFactor 2](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/RFactor_2)

Verstappen competed in the [24 Hours of Le Mans Virtual](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/24_Hours_of_Le_Mans_Virtual) on *[rFactor 2](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/RFactor_2)* in [2020](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2020_24_Hours_of_Le_Mans_Virtual), [2022](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2022_24_Hours_of_Le_Mans_Virtual) and [2023](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2023_24_Hours_of_Le_Mans_Virtual?action=edit\&redlink=1)—hosted by [Automobile Club de l'Ouest](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Automobile_Club_de_l'Ouest) and the [FIA World Endurance Championship](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/FIA_World_Endurance_Championship)—as well as two further rounds of the [2022–23 Le Mans Virtual Series](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2022%E2%80%9323_Le_Mans_Virtual_Series) in the [LMP2](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/LMP2) class.[^cite_note-ATK-418] He described the former competition as a "clown show" after a technical issue saw him retire from the lead of the 2023 edition,[^cite_note-422] subsequently finishing runner-up in the overall championship to [Porsche Coanda](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porsche_in_motorsport).[^cite_note-ATK-418]

## Verstappen Racing

Thierry Vermeulen driving the Verstappen.com Racing Ferrari 296 GT3 in the 2024 Deutsche Tourenwagen Masters at the Red Bull Ring

In 2022, Verstappen founded the [eponymous](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Eponymous) racing team Verstappen.com Racing.

Verstappen founded the [eponymous](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Eponymous) racing team **Verstappen.com Racing** in 2022, supporting his father Jos in [rallying](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rallying) and [Thierry Vermeulen](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Thierry_Vermeulen) in the [ADAC GT Masters](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/ADAC_GT_Masters).[^cite_note-423][^cite_note-424] The following year, the project expanded to [GT World Challenge Europe Sprint Cup](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2023_GT_World_Challenge_Europe_Sprint_Cup) and the [Deutsche Tourenwagen Masters](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2023_Deutsche_Tourenwagen_Masters), linking up with [Ferrari](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ferrari) customer [Emil Frey Racing](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Emil_Frey_Racing).[^cite_note-425] In 2025, in conjunction with this effort, Verstappen.com made its debut as a standalone team in the [Endurance Cup](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2025_GT_World_Challenge_Europe_Endurance_Cup), fielding a [2 Seas Motorsport](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2_Seas_Motorsport)–run [Aston Martin Vantage AMR GT3 Evo](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Aston_Martin_Vantage_\(2018\)#GT3) for Vermeulen, [Harry King](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Harry_King_\(racing_driver\)) and [sim racing](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sim_racing) convert [Chris Lulham](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Chris_Lulham);[^cite_note-426] they took the Gold Cup victory at the [24 Hours of Spa](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2025_24_Hours_of_Spa).[^cite_note-427]

2026 saw the team rebrand to **Verstappen Racing** as it switched to [Mercedes-AMG](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mercedes-AMG) machinery and graduated to the Pro class, with a season-long [GT World Challenge Europe](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2026_GT_World_Challenge_Europe) entry with 2 Seas Motorsport, fielding Lulham and [Daniel Juncadella](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Daniel_Juncadella) in the Sprint Cup, with [Jules Gounon](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jules_Gounon) joining for the Endurance Cup rounds.[^cite_note-Verstappen2SeasMerc-428] In March 2026, it was announced that both the team and Verstappen would make their [24 Hours of Nürburgring](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2026_24_Hours_of_N%C3%BCrburgring) debut, partnering with [Winward Racing](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Winward_Racing). Verstappen drove the No. 3 Red Bull–liveried [Mercedes-AMG GT3 Evo](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mercedes-AMG_GT#AMG_GT3_Evo_\(2020%E2%80%932029\)) alongside Gounon, Juncadella and [Lucas Auer](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lucas_Auer).[^cite_note-429] Verstappen first forayed into [driver development](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Driver_development_programme) that July, signing Belgian prodigy [Dries Van Langendonck](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dries_Van_Langendonck) as a junior member of his team.[^cite_note-430]

## Personal life



Verstappen's former aircraft (PH-DTF) at [Luton Airport](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Luton_Airport) in 2022

Verstappen holds [dual](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dual_citizenship) Dutch and Belgian citizenship, and decided to compete with a Dutch racing licence, as his father [Jos](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jos_Verstappen) did. He stated in 2014 that he "feels more Dutch", having spent more time with his father than with his mother during his childhood, as well as his upbringing in [Maaseik](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Maaseik), a Belgian town at the Dutch border.[^cite_note-nationality2-9] The following year, Verstappen added that he "actually only lived in Belgium to sleep", and that he "was raised as a Dutch person and that's how \[he feels]".[^cite_note-431] In 2022, Verstappen stated that he appreciates both sides and is "half-half at the end of the day".[^cite_note-432] In addition to his [native](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dutch_language) Dutch, he is fluent in English and [German](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/German_language), with limited proficiency in [French](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/French_language).[^cite_note-433]

He competed in Formula One for over a season before obtaining a road driving licence on his 18th birthday.[^cite_note-434] Verstappen moved to [Monaco](19261.md) in October 2015 where he has lived ever since.[^cite_note-GPNov15-435] In November 2020, Verstappen bought a [Dassault Falcon 900EX](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dassault_Falcon_900) aircraft from [Virgin Galactic](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Virgin_Galactic). The aircraft, [registered](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Aircraft_registration) PH-DTF, was operated by Exxaero.[^cite_note-Falcon-436] In January 2025, he acquired a Mangusta GranSport 33 yacht, named *Unleash the Lion*.[^cite_note-Yacht-437] The following month, he sold the Falcon 900EX and purchased a [Dassault Falcon 8X](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dassault_Falcon_8X), registered PH-UTL, which is also operated by Exxaero.[^cite_note-Falcon8x-438]

Verstappen is a supporter of the [Barcelona](68187.md) and [PSV Eindhoven](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/PSV_Eindhoven) football clubs.[^cite_note-439] In September 2022, he was appointed an Officer of the [Order of Orange-Nassau](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Order_of_Orange-Nassau) in recognition of his racing achievements.[^cite_note-auto-440] Verstappen has been dating Brazilian-Dutch model [Kelly Piquet](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kelly_Piquet), daughter of three-time World Drivers' Champion [Nelson Piquet](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nelson_Piquet), since October 2020.[^cite_note-441][^cite_note-442] They had their first daughter in April 2025.[^cite_note-443]

#### Formula One records

Main article: [List of career achievements by Max Verstappen § Records](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_career_achievements_by_Max_Verstappen#Records)

Verstappen made his Formula One debut at the [2015 Australian Grand Prix](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2015_Australian_Grand_Prix), and has since gone on to hold [over 90 records](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_career_achievements_by_Max_Verstappen#Records) in Formula One, including [most wins in a season](13599687.md#Most_wins_in_a_season), [most podium finishes in a season](13599687.md#Most_podium_finishes_in_a_season), [most consecutive wins](13599687.md#Most_consecutive_wins), and [most consecutive pole positions](13599687.md#Most_consecutive_pole_positions).

[^cite_note-3]: &#x20;Including most consecutive wins (6), youngest winner (16 years, 216 days), and youngest polesitter (16 years, 215 days).

[^cite_note-366]: &#x20;Including the [British Grand Prix](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2021_British_Grand_Prix), where Hamilton made contact with Verstappen, which resulted in a high-speed crash; Red Bull chief technology officer [Adrian Newey](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Adrian_Newey) stated that "I think Max's \[crash] at Silverstone was the last one where we thought, 'Is he okay? Is he badly hurt in there?'";<sup>[\[360\]](#cite_note-363)</sup> Verstappen later revealed that he raced with severe blurred vision after the crash. He described seeing "wavy" or "blurry" images, particularly on bumpy circuits like [COTA](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Circuit_of_the_Americas) at the [2021 United States Grand Prix](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2021_United_States_Grand_Prix), where he almost stopped.<sup>[\[361\]](#cite_note-364)</sup><sup>[\[362\]](#cite_note-365)</sup> The other notable incident occurred at the [Italian Grand Prix](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2021_Italian_Grand_Prix), where Verstappen's car was stacked on top of Hamilton's after a kerb sent him upward.

[^cite_note-nationality2-9]: &#x20;Cornelissen, Marc (17 August 2014). ["Geen rijbewijs, wel in de F1"](http://www.nieuwsblad.be/sportwereld/cnt/dmf20140816_01219423) \[No driver's licence, yet in F1]. *Nieuwsblad.be* (in Dutch). [Archived](https://web.archive.org/web/20150308132510/http://www.nieuwsblad.be/sportwereld/cnt/dmf20140816_01219423) from the original on 8 March 2015. Retrieved 12 December 2014. Max heeft een Belgische moeder, is geboren in Hasselt, woont in Bree en reist met een Belgisch paspoort. Genetisch en cultureel is hij Belgischer dan de gemiddelde Rode Duivel. Maar racen doet hij met een Nederlandse licentie. 'Ik heb heel mijn leven in België gewoond, maar ik voel meer Nederlander. Door al dat karten trek ik meer met mijn vader op dan met mijn moeder. Ik zit gewoon altijd tussen de Nederlanders. \[Max has a Belgian mother, was born in Hasselt, lives in Bree, and travels with a Belgian passport. From a genetic and cultural perspective, he's more Belgian than the average Red Devil. Yet he competes with a Dutch racing licence. 'I have lived in Belgium my entire life, but I consider myself more Dutch. As a result of my karting activities I spend more time with my father than with my mother. I'm always surrounded by Dutch people.']
